\"example.com\",\n46\t\t\t\twantTLD: \"com\",\n47\t\t\t},\n48\t\t\t// #5: Multi-level subdomain\n49\t\t\t{\n50\t\t\t\tname:    \"multi-level subdomain\",\n51\t\t\t\tinput: expected\n\t\twantDomain  string // if success expected\n\t\tdescription string\n\t}{\n\t\t// #45: PSL private suffix - these are non-ICANN domains\n\t\t// The PSL returns icann=false for private // #3: Multi-level TLD with subdomain\n\t\t\"münchen.de\",            // #4: IDN input (converts to punycode)\n\t\t\"\",                      // #5: Empty string\n\t\t\"example..com\",          // #6: Empty label\n\t\t\"-example.com\",          // #7: Leading hyphen\n\t\t\"http://example.com\",    // #8: URL scheme\n\t\tstrings.Repeat(\"a\", 64) + \".com\", // #9: Label too long (64 chars)\n\t}\n\tfor _, seed := range seeds {\n\t\tf.Add(seed)\n\t}\n\n\tf.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, input string) {\n\t\t// Property 1: Must never panic (the test framework handles this)\n\t\tresult, err !strings.Contains(result.Domain, \".\") {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"valid domain missing dot: %q\", result.Domain)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Property 3: Each label ≤63 characters\n\t\t\tlabels := result.Domain)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\nfunc TestParse_EffectiveTLDPlusOne(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname  string\n\t\tinput string\n\t\twant  string\n\t}{\n\t\t{name: \"com\", *httptest.Server) *RDAPClient {\n844\t\t// Create bootstrap that points to test server\n845\t\tbootstrap := &BootstrapManager{\n846\t\t\tservers: map[string]string{\n847\t\t\t\t\"com\": server.URL + \"/\",\n848\t\t\t},\n849\t\t\tupdated: time.Now(),\n850\t\t}\n851\t\n852\t\tallowlist := NewAllowList([]string{server.URL})\n853\t\n854\t\treturn NewRDAPClient(RDAPClientConfig{\n855\t\t\tHTTPClient: \"domain-check-test/1.0\",\n860\t\t})\n861\t}\n862\t\n863\t// FuzzParseRDAPResponse fuzzes RDAP response parsing with random inputs.\n864\t// Properties verified:\n865\t//   - Never panics on any input\n866\t//   - Valid results have non-empty domain (when ldhName present)\n867\t//   - Deterministic: same input always produces same output\n868\tfunc FuzzParseRDAPResponse(f *testing.F) {\n869\t\t// Seed corpus: empty + inline JSON + fixture files\n870\t\tf.Add([]byte(\"\"))\n871\t\tf.Add([]byte(`{\"objectClassName\": \"domain\", \"ldhName\": \"example.com\"}`))\n872\t\tf.Add([]byte(`{\"objectClassName\": \"error\"}`))\n873\t\tf.Add([]byte(\"not json at all\"))\n874\t\tf.Add([]byte(`{\"ldhName\": \"EXAMPLE.COM\", \"status\": [\"active\"]}`))\n875\t\n876\t\t// Add fixture files as seed corpus\n877\t\tfixtureDir := filepath.Join(\"testdata\", \"rdap\")\n878\t\tfixtures := the full Check flow with a mock server.\nfunc TestRDAPClientCheck(t *testing.T) {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tt.Run(\"registered domain\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, \"/domain/example.com\") {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"unexpected path: %s\", r.URL.Path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/rdap+json\")\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n\t\t\t_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{\n\t\t\t\t\"objectClassName\": \"domain\",\n\t\t\t\t\"ldhName\": \"example.com\",\n\t\t\t\t\"entities\": [{\"ldhName\": \"Test Registrar\", \"roles\": [\"registrar\"]}],\n\t\t\t\t\"events\": [\n\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"registration\", \"eventDate\": \"2020-01-01T00:00:00Z\"},\n\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"expiration\", \"eventDate\": \"2025-01-01T00:00:00Z\"}\n\t\t\t\t]\n\t\t\t}`))\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"example.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif result.Available {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected domain to be registered\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Registration == nil || result.Registration.Registrar != \"Test Registrar\" {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected registrar, got: %+v\", result.Registration)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"available domain\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"available123.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !result.Available {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected domain to be available\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Registration != nil && result.Registration.Registrar != \"\" {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected no registrar for available domain, got: %s\", result.Registration.Registrar)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"rate limited\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusTooManyRequests)\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"ratelimited.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif result.Error == \"\" {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected error for rate limited response\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"empty body on 404\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)\n\t\t\t// No body, like Verisign\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"empty404.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !result.Available {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected domain to be available (empty 404)\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"invalid domain input\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"should not make request for invalid domain\")\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\t_, err := client.Check(ctx, \"http://evil.com\")\n\t\tif err == nil {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected error for domain with URL characters\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\n// newTestRDAPClient creates an RDAPClient configured for testing with a mock server.\nfunc newTestRDAPClient(server *httptest.Server) *RDAPClient {\n\t// Create bootstrap that points to test server\n\tbootstrap := &BootstrapManager{\n\t\tservers: map[string]string{\n\t\t\t\"com\": server.URL + \"/\",\n\t\t},\n\t\tupdated: time.Now(),\n\t}\n\n\tallowlist := NewAllowList([]string{server.URL})\n\n\treturn NewRDAPClient(RDAPClientConfig{\n\t\tHTTPClient: server.Client(),\n\t\tBootstrap:  bootstrap,\n\t\tRateLimit:  NewRateLimiter(),\n\t\tAllowList:  allowlist,\n\t\tUserAgent:  \"domain-check-test/1.0\",\n\t})\n}\n\n// FuzzParseRDAPResponse fuzzes RDAP response parsing with random inputs.\n// Properties verified:\n//   - Never panics on any input\n//   - Valid results have non-empty domain (when ldhName present)\n//   - Deterministic: same input always produces same output\nfunc FuzzParseRDAPResponse(f *testing.F) {\n\t// Seed corpus: empty + inline JSON + fixture files\n\tf.Add([]byte(\"\"))\n\tf.Add([]byte(`{\"objectClassName\": \"domain\", \"ldhName\": \"example.com\"}`))\n\tf.Add([]byte(`{\"objectClassName\": \"error\"}`))\n\tf.Add([]byte(\"not json at all\"))\n\tf.Add([]byte(`{\"ldhName\": \"EXAMPLE.COM\", \"status\": [\"active\"]}`))\n\n\t// Add fixture files as seed corpus\n\tfixtureDir := filepath.Join(\"testdata\", \"rdap\")\n\tfixtures := []string{\n\t\t\"verisign-google.com.json\",\n\t\t\"pir-wikipedia.org.json\",\n\t\t\"google-web.dev.json\",\n\t\t\"centralnic-example.xyz.json\",\n\t\t\"denic-example.de.json\",\n\t\t\"nominet-bbc.uk.json\",\n\t\t\"verisign-404.txt\",\n\t\t\"google-429.json\",\n\t}\n\tfor _, fixture := range fixtures {\n\t\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(fixtureDir, fixture))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tf.Fatalf(\"failed to read fixture %s: %v\", fixture, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tf.Add(data)\n\t}\n\n\tf.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, data []byte) {\n\t\t// Property 1: Must never panic (test framework handles this)\n\t\treg := parseRDAPBody(data)\n\t\tif reg == nil
